# Quickstart

This page gets you from install to a useful first pi session.

## Install

Pi is distributed as an npm package:

```bash
npm install -g @earendil-works/pi-coding-agent
```

Then start pi in the project directory you want it to work on:

```bash
cd /path/to/project
pi
```

## Authenticate

Pi can use subscription providers through `/login`, or API-key providers through environment variables or the auth file.

### Option 1: subscription login

Start pi and run:

```text
/login
```

Then select a provider. Built-in subscription logins include Claude Pro/Max, ChatGPT Plus/Pro (Codex), and GitHub Copilot.

### Option 2: API key

Set an API key before launching pi:

```bash
export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-ant-...
pi
```

You can also run `/login` and select an API-key provider to store the key in `~/.pi/agent/auth.json`.

See [Providers](/docs/latest/providers) for all supported providers, environment variables, and cloud-provider setup.

## First session

Once pi starts, type a request and press Enter:

```text
Summarize this repository and tell me how to run its checks.
```

By default, pi gives the model four tools:

*   `read` - read files
*   `write` - create or overwrite files
*   `edit` - patch files
*   `bash` - run shell commands

Additional built-in read-only tools (`grep`, `find`, `ls`) are available through tool options. Pi runs in your current working directory and can modify files there. Use git or another checkpointing workflow if you want easy rollback.

## Give pi project instructions

Pi loads context files at startup. Add an `AGENTS.md` file to tell it how to work in a project:

```markdown
# Project Instructions

- Run `npm run check` after code changes.
- Do not run production migrations locally.
- Keep responses concise.
```

Pi loads:

*   `~/.pi/agent/AGENTS.md` for global instructions
*   `AGENTS.md` or `CLAUDE.md` from parent directories and the current directory

Restart pi, or run `/reload`, after changing context files.

## Common things to try

### Reference files

Type `@` in the editor to fuzzy-search files, or pass files on the command line:

```bash
pi @README.md "Summarize this"
pi @src/app.ts @src/app.test.ts "Review these together"
```

Images can be pasted with Ctrl+V (Alt+V on Windows) or dragged into supported terminals.

### Run shell commands

In interactive mode:

```text
!npm run lint
```

The command output is sent to the model. Use `!!command` to run a command without adding its output to the model context.

### Switch models

Use `/model` or Ctrl+L to choose a model. Use Shift+Tab to cycle thinking level. Use Ctrl+P / Shift+Ctrl+P to cycle through scoped models.

### Continue later

Sessions are saved automatically:

```bash
pi -c                  # Continue most recent session
pi -r                  # Browse previous sessions
pi --session <path|id> # Open a specific session
```

Inside pi, use `/resume`, `/new`, `/tree`, `/fork`, and `/clone` to manage sessions.

### Non-interactive mode

For one-shot prompts:

```b

…[truncated — use search() for full content]
```

Got it. The error is from the **claude.ai subscription** — `pi` is authenticated via `/login` with your Claude Pro/Max account, and you've hit the "extra usage" cap.

Two options to fix it:

**Option 1 — Add more usage credits** (quickest)
Go to [claude.ai/settings/usage](https://claude.ai/settings/usage) and top up.

**Option 2 — Switch to a direct API key** (bypasses the cap)
```bash
export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-ant-...
pi -p "Summarize this codebase"
```
Direct API keys use pay-as-you-go Anthropic billing, not the claude.ai plan cap. You can make it permanent by running `/login` inside `pi` and selecting the API key provider.

Option 2 is the more reliable long-term fix if you're doing heavy non-interactive usage with `-p`.

> DEVELOPER

I do have, check youself, just 13% used

> AGENT

The key distinction: "extra usage" on claude.ai is a **separate purchased add-on pool**, not the same as your base subscription message quota. You could have 87% of your base messages remaining but zero extra usage credits.

The 13% you're seeing is likely your base subscription usage. The error fires when the **extra usage credit balance** (a separate purchasable pool) is depleted or was never added.

Want me to open claude.ai/settings/usage in your browser to check if the "Extra usage" section shows a separate balance?

> DEVELOPER

Error: 400 {"type":"error","error":{"type":"invalid_request_error","message":"Your credit balance is too low to access the Anthropic API. Please go to Plans & Billing to upgrade or purchase
 credits."},"request_id":"REDACTED" is this different from my claude pro max plan?

> AGENT

Yes, these are **two completely separate billing systems**:

| | Claude Pro/Max | Anthropic API |
|---|---|---|
| Where | claude.ai | console.anthropic.com |
| How billed | Monthly subscription | Pay-as-you-go credits |
| Used by | claude.ai browser/apps | API keys (`sk-ant-...`) |

The second error — **"Your credit balance is too low to access the Anthropic API"** — means `pi` is now using an `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` environment variable, and that API account has no credits. Your Claude Pro/Max subscription does **not** carry over to the API.

**Check which auth `pi` is using:**
```bash
echo $ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
```

If that prints a key, `pi` is billing against your Anthropic API account (empty). You have two options:

1. **Use your Claude Pro/Max subscription** — run `pi`, then `/login` → select Claude Pro/Max
2. **Buy API credits** — go to [console.anthropic.com](https://console.anthropic.com) → Billing → add credits (separate from your claude.ai subscription)

Option 1 is the easiest if you already have Pro/Max.

# Providers

Pi supports subscription-based providers via OAuth and API key providers via environment variables or auth file. For each provider, pi knows all available models. The list is updated with every pi release.

## Table of Contents

*   [Subscriptions](#subscriptions)
*   [API Keys](#api-keys)
*   [Auth File](#auth-file)
*   [Cloud Providers](#cloud-providers)
*   [Custom Providers](#custom-providers)
*   [Resolution Order](#resolution-order)

## Subscriptions

Use `/login` in interactive mode, then select a provider:

*   ChatGPT Plus/Pro (Codex)
*   Claude Pro/Max
*   GitHub Copilot

Use `/logout` to clear credentials. Tokens are stored in `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` and auto-refresh when expired.

### OpenAI Codex

*   Requires ChatGPT Plus or Pro subscription
*   Officially endorsed by OpenAI: [Codex for OSS](https://developers.openai.com/community/codex-for-oss)

### Claude Pro/Max

Anthropic subscription auth is active for Claude Pro/Max accounts. Third-party harness usage draws from [extra usage](https://claude.ai/settings/usage) and is billed per token, not against Claude plan limits.

### GitHub Copilot

*   Press Enter for github.com, or enter your GitHub Enterprise Server domain
*   If you get "model not supported", enable it in VS Code: Copilot Chat → model selector → select model → "Enable"

## API Keys

### Environment Variables or Auth File

Use `/login` in interactive mode and select a provider to store an API key in `auth.json`, or set credentials via environment variable:

```bash
export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-ant-...
pi
```

| Provider | Environment Variable | `auth.json` key |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Anthropic | `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | `anthropic` |
| Azure OpenAI Responses | `AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY` | `azure-openai-responses` |
| OpenAI | `OPENAI_API_KEY` | `openai` |
| DeepSeek | `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | `deepseek` |
| Google Gemini | `GEMINI_API_KEY` | `google` |
| Mistral | `MISTRAL_API_KEY` | `mistral` |
| Groq | `GROQ_API_KEY` | `groq` |
| Cerebras | `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` | `cerebras` |
| Cloudflare AI Gateway | `CLOUDFLARE_API_KEY` (+ `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`, `CLOUDFLARE_GATEWAY_ID`) | `cloudflare-ai-gateway` |
| Cloudflare Workers AI | `CLOUDFLARE_API_KEY` (+ `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`) | `cloudflare-workers-ai` |
| xAI | `XAI_API_KEY` | `xai` |
| OpenRouter | `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` | `openrouter` |
| Vercel AI Gateway | `AI_GATEWAY_API_KEY` | `vercel-ai-gateway` |
| ZAI | `ZAI_API_KEY` | `zai` |
| OpenCode Zen | `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | `opencode` |
| OpenCode Go | `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | `opencode-go` |
| Hugging Face | `HF_TOKEN` | `huggingface` |
| Fireworks | `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` | `fireworks` |
| Together AI | `TOGETHER_API_KEY` | `together` |

## extra usage billed per token

--- [pi.dev providers] ---
### Providers > Subscriptions > Claude Pro/Max

### Claude Pro/Max

Anthropic subscription auth is active for Claude Pro/Max accounts. Third-party harness usage draws from [extra usage](https://claude.ai/settings/usage) and is billed per token, not against Claude plan limits.
```

> AGENT

Found the key detail. Yes, but there's a catch — the docs say:

> *Third-party harness usage draws from **extra usage** and is billed per token, not against Claude plan limits.*

So `pi` with Claude Pro/Max uses a **separate "extra usage" pool** at claude.ai/settings/usage — your regular subscription messages don't apply. That's why you saw the first error despite only 13% used.

The second error ("API credit balance too low") appeared because you have `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` set in your environment, which **takes priority** over the subscription login per pi's resolution order:
```
1. --api-key flag
2. auth.json (OAuth token from /login)  ← subscription lives here
3. Environment variable                 ← ANTHROPIC_API_KEY overrides it
```

**To use your Pro/Max subscription:**
```bash
unset ANTHROPIC_API_KEY   # remove the env var so subscription takes priority
pi
# then /login → Claude Pro/Max (if not already logged in)
```

Then go to [claude.ai/settings/usage](https://claude.ai/settings/usage) and purchase some "extra usage" credits — even a small amount since it's pay-per-token.

> DEVELOPER

so i need to pay extra to use pi? why?

> AGENT

It's not pi's decision — it's **Anthropic's policy**. Your Pro/Max subscription gives you a message quota on **claude.ai only**. When any third-party tool (pi, Claude Code, etc.) connects via your subscription's OAuth, Anthropic routes that through a separate metered pool ("extra usage") rather than your subscription quota.

The reasoning: your subscription quota is rate-limited and tied to claude.ai's interface. Letting every third-party tool drain it would be hard to control.

**Alternatives that don't require extra payment:**

1. **Use Claude Code instead** — Anthropic's own CLI (`claude`) is included with your Pro/Max plan and draws from your subscription quota directly, not extra usage.

2. **Use a different provider in pi** — pi supports GitHub Copilot, which has its own API access included in the Copilot subscription:
   ```
   /login → GitHub Copilot
   ```

3. **Use OpenRouter** — aggregates many models, often cheaper than direct Anthropic API credits:
   ```bash
   export OPENROUTER_API_KEY=...
   pi
   ```

4. **Use Gemini** — Google gives generous free API credits:
   ```bash
   export GEMINI_API_KEY=...
   pi
   ```

If you just want to summarize and work on the Raven codebase, Claude Code (which you're already using right now) can do everything pi does.

> AGENT

Good foundation to work from. Let me start at the top.

**Question 1:** Who is Raven's primary customer — individuals (B2C), teams/companies (B2B), or developers who self-host it?

My recommendation: **B2B teams**, given the edge deployment story (Raspberry Pi, on-prem), compliance posture (GDPR/SOC2), and the LiveKit voice + AI worker stack — that's enterprise-grade infrastructure that individuals rarely care about.

> DEVELOPER

i agree

> AGENT

**Question 2:** What is the core value Raven delivers to a team — is it primarily **AI-assisted communication** (voice + chat with AI), **self-hosted data sovereignty** (your data on your infra, not a cloud vendor), or **both equally**?

My recommendation: **self-hosted data sovereignty with AI on top** — the edge deployment on Raspberry Pi and GDPR/SOC2 compliance posture signals the primary differentiator is "your data stays with you." The AI features are the premium layer on top of that.

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

**Question 3:** What are your current monthly expenses? Walk me through the main buckets — infra (AWS/servers), CI, any SaaS tools, domain/CDN, etc.

My recommendation: list them all so we can set a floor on what the pricing must recover before we even think about margin.

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

I need actual numbers here — "yes" doesn't give me a floor to work with. What are you spending monthly, roughly? Even ballpark figures work:

- AWS / hosting: ~$___/month?
- CI (GitHub Actions minutes): ~$___/month?
- SaaS tools (CodeRabbit, PostHog, etc.): ~$___/month?
- Domain / Cloudflare: ~$___/month?
- LLM API costs (if any): ~$___/month?

> DEVELOPER

15-20k inr

> AGENT

₹15–20k/month (~$180–240 USD) all-in. Good, that's the floor.

**Question 4:** Is that all-in (infra + CI + tools + everything), or are there other costs not included — like your own time, LLM API costs per customer, or hardware?

My recommendation: treat your time as a cost too even if you're not paying yourself yet — otherwise you'll underprice and burn out.

> DEVELOPER

well my own time then, cost will be 4-5 lakh inr per month

> AGENT

So total cost is ~₹4.15–5.2 lakh/month (~$5,000–6,200 USD). That's the real floor.

**Question 5:** How many paying teams are you realistically targeting in your **first 12 months**? And what size are these teams — 5-person startups, 20-person SMBs, or 50+ person enterprises?

My recommendation: **5–15 teams of 10–30 people** is a realistic year-one target for a solo founder with a self-hosted B2B product. More than that requires sales infrastructure you don't have yet.

> DEVELOPER

ya

> AGENT

So 5–15 teams, 10–30 people each. Let me sanity-check the math:

To cover ₹5.2 lakh/month and make **some** profit (say 30% margin), you need ~₹6.75 lakh/month revenue.

- At **5 teams**: ₹1.35 lakh/team/month (~$1,600/team/month)
- At **10 teams**: ₹67,500/team/month (~$800/team/month)
- At **15 teams**: ₹45,000/team/month (~$540/team/month)

**Question 6:** Does a per-team flat fee feel right, or do you want **per-seat pricing** (per user per month)? 

My recommendation: **per-seat** in the ₹1,500–2,500/seat/month range. A 20-person team at ₹2,000/seat = ₹40,000/month. At 10 teams that's ₹4 lakh — close to break-even at year one, with room to grow as teams expand. Per-seat also aligns your revenue with the value teams get (more users = more value = more revenue).

> DEVELOPER

cool

> AGENT

**Question 7:** Do you want a **free tier** to drive adoption, or **paid-only from day one**?

My recommendation: **open-source core, paid for managed hosting or enterprise features**. Let teams self-host for free — this builds trust and community. Charge for:
- **Managed cloud hosting** (you run it for them)
- **Enterprise features** (SSO, audit logs, SLA, priority support)

This is the classic open-core model and fits perfectly with Raven's self-hosted story. Teams that can't manage infra themselves become paying customers.

> DEVELOPER

ya correct

> AGENT

**Question 8:** Which features belong in the **free open-source core** vs. the **paid tiers**? 

Looking at Raven's stack, here's how I'd split it — does this feel right?

| Tier | Features | Price |
|------|----------|-------|
| **Open Source** (self-host) | Core chat, voice (LiveKit), file storage, basic AI | Free |
| **Pro** (managed cloud) | Everything + managed infra, updates, backups, uptime SLA | ₹1,500–2,000/seat/month |
| **Enterprise** (self-host or cloud) | Pro + SSO, audit logs, GDPR compliance reports, priority support, custom SLA | ₹3,000–4,000/seat/month |

My recommendation: **keep AI features in Pro and above** — that's your LLM/vector search/voice AI stack, which costs you money per usage. Don't give it away in the free tier.

> DEVELOPER

works

> AGENT

**Question 9:** For the AI features in Pro/Enterprise — does the customer **bring their own LLM API key** (BYOK), or does **Raven provide LLM access** bundled into the seat price?

My recommendation: **BYOK for self-hosted, Raven-provided for managed cloud** (Pro). This way:
- Self-hosters pay zero to you for AI compute — their risk
- Managed Pro customers pay you per seat, and you bake in an LLM cost estimate per seat (e.g. assume 10k tokens/user/day) when setting the ₹1,500–2,000 price point

If you bundle LLM costs without modelling usage, a single power-user team can flip your margin negative. Do you have any sense of average LLM usage per user per day right now?

> DEVELOPER

byok

> AGENT

BYOK across the board — clean, no LLM cost risk for you.

**Question 10:** Monthly billing or annual with a discount? 

My recommendation: **offer both — monthly at rack rate, annual at 20% discount** (2 months free). Annual plans dramatically improve your cash flow — getting ₹4.8 lakh upfront from one 20-person Pro team is far better than collecting ₹40k/month. For a solo founder covering ₹5 lakh/month in costs, predictable annual ARR is survival.

> DEVELOPER

yup

> AGENT

**Question 11:** Free trial before paying — yes or no, and how long?

My recommendation: **14-day free trial, no credit card required**, for managed Pro only. Self-hosted is already free forever, so the trial is purely to let teams evaluate the managed experience. No credit card upfront reduces friction and fits the B2B evaluation cycle — most procurement decisions take 1–2 weeks anyway.

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

**Question 12:** Minimum seat count per plan — or can a single user sign up for Pro?

My recommendation: **minimum 5 seats for Pro, minimum 20 seats for Enterprise**. Raven is a team collaboration tool — a 1-person "team" is a support burden with no meaningful revenue (₹7,500–10,000/month for 5 seats vs ₹1,500 for 1). It also prevents people from gaming the free tier indefinitely with tiny paid plans.

> DEVELOPER

yeah

> AGENT

**Question 13:** When the 14-day trial ends — does the account **auto-convert to paid** (card on file required at trial start), or does it **lock and require manual upgrade**?

My recommendation: **lock and prompt, no auto-charge**. B2B buyers need procurement approval — auto-charging without explicit consent kills trust and can violate procurement policies at larger companies. Lock access, send a clear email 3 days before expiry and on expiry day, and give a 7-day grace period to complete purchase before data is archived.

> DEVELOPER

ya

> AGENT

**Question 14:** If a team adds seats mid-cycle — do you **prorate** the charge for the remainder of the month, or bill the full seat price from next cycle?

My recommendation: **prorate immediately** (charge for days remaining in cycle). Delaying to next cycle means teams game the system by adding seats on day 29. Immediate prorated billing is the industry standard (Stripe/Hyperswitch both handle this natively) and feels fair to customers.

> DEVELOPER

  "content": "# ADR 0001 — Open-Core Pricing Model\n\n**Status:** Accepted  \n**Date:** 2026-05-15\n\n## Context\n\nRaven is a self-hosted B2B collaboration platform with AI features. We needed a pricing model that drives adoption while generating revenue sufficient to cover ~₹5.2 lakh/month in costs (infra + founder time).\n\n## Decision\n\nAdopt an **open-core model**: the core platform is open-source and free to self-host; revenue comes from managed cloud hosting (Pro) and enterprise features (Enterprise).\n\n## Alternatives Considered\n\n- **Fully paid SaaS only** — eliminates the self-host audience but loses the primary differentiator (data sovereignty). Teams evaluating self-hosted solutions won't pay before they can try.\n- **Freemium SaaS** — gives away managed hosting for free, which costs Raven money per user with no recovery path.\n- **Usage-based only** — unpredictable revenue; difficult to model against fixed monthly costs.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Open-sourcing the core is a long-term commitment — reversing it would damage community trust.\n- Managed Pro tier must be priced to cover infra + support overhead per team, not just marginal compute.\n- Enterprise tier requires SSO/audit log implementation before it can be sold — these are gates, not upsells.\n- BYOK across all tiers eliminates LLM cost risk entirely; customers own their AI spend.\n"
}

> AGENT

**Question 15:** How does an OSS self-hosted team **upgrade to Pro** managed? Do they migrate their data to your cloud, or do you offer a managed deployment on their own infra?

My recommendation: **both options — Raven-hosted cloud OR managed on their infra (bring-your-own-cloud)**. "Managed on customer infra" (you deploy and maintain it on their servers/VPC) is a strong Enterprise upsell and lets privacy-strict teams stay compliant without managing ops themselves. This is your conversion funnel: OSS → Pro cloud → Enterprise managed.

> DEVELOPER

agreed

> AGENT

**Question 16:** Enterprise deals — **self-serve signup** or **sales-led** (you talk to them first)?

My recommendation: **sales-led for Enterprise, self-serve for Pro**. Enterprise buyers (20+ seats, SSO, SLAs) never buy without a conversation — they have procurement, legal, and security reviews. Your time is better spent on 3–4 Enterprise calls/month than building a self-serve Enterprise checkout flow. Pro at 5+ seats can be fully self-serve via Hyperswitch.

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

**Question 17:** Support model per tier — how do customers reach you, and how fast do you respond?

My recommendation:

| Tier | Channel | SLA |
|------|---------|-----|
| **OSS** | GitHub Issues only | Best effort, no guarantee |
| **Pro** | Email / Discord | 48h response, business days |
| **Enterprise** | Dedicated Slack channel | 4h response, business days |

This protects your time — free users get community support, paying customers get guaranteed response. A dedicated Slack channel per Enterprise customer is cheap to set up and feels premium.

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

**Question 18:** After the 7-day grace period expires — how long before customer data is **permanently deleted**?

My recommendation: **archive immediately on day 22 (lock day + 7), delete permanently after 30 days**. Send one final warning email on day 21. This gives customers a clear, fair window while limiting your storage costs for non-paying accounts. Document it explicitly in your Terms of Service — B2B buyers will check.

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

**Question 19:** Refund policy — do you offer refunds for annual plans if a customer wants to cancel mid-year?

My recommendation: **pro-rated refund for unused months on annual plans, no refunds on monthly**. Example: a team pays ₹4.8 lakh upfront for a year, cancels after 3 months — refund 9 months = ₹3.6 lakh. It sounds painful but it's the only policy that passes B2B procurement scrutiny. Companies won't sign annual contracts without a cancellation clause. Monthly plans are already low-commitment, so no refunds there.

> DEVELOPER

cool

> AGENT

**Question 20 (last one):** Enterprise contracts — minimum **annual commitment only**, or allow monthly?

My recommendation: **annual-only for Enterprise**. Monthly Enterprise would mean SSO setup, security reviews, and dedicated Slack support for a customer who could churn next month. The procurement process for 20+ seat deals takes weeks anyway — anyone going through that is committed to a year. Annual-only also simplifies your billing and forecasting significantly.

> DEVELOPER

yeah

### Triage docs
# Triage docs

### Triage Labels
# Triage Labels

The skills speak in terms of five canonical triage roles. This file maps those roles to the actual label strings used in this repo's issue tracker.

| Label in mattpocock/skills | Label in our tracker | Meaning                                  |
| -------------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
| `needs-triage`             | `needs-triage`       | Maintainer needs to evaluate this issue  |
| `needs-info`               | `needs-info`         | Waiting on reporter for more information |
| `ready-for-agent`          | `ready-for-agent`    | Fully specified, ready for an AFK agent  |
| `ready-for-human`          | `ready-for-human`    | Requires human implementation            |
| `wontfix`                  | `wontfix`            | Will not be actioned                     |

When a skill mentions a role (e.g. "apply the AFK-ready triage label"), use the corresponding label string from this table.

Edit the right-hand column to match whatever vocabulary you actually use.

> Note: `wontfix` already exists in `ravencloak-org/Raven`. The other four labels will be created by the `triage` skill on first use (via `gh label create`).

// CreateSubscriptionRequest is the payload for POST /api/v1/billing/subscribe.
type CreateSubscriptionRequest struct {
	PlanID string `json:"plan_id" binding:"required"`
}

// CreatePaymentIntentRequest is the payload for creating a payment intent.
type CreatePaymentIntentRequest struct {
	Amount   int64  `json:"amount" binding:"required,gt=0"`
	Currency string `json:"currency" binding:"required,len=3"`
}

// HyperswitchWebhookPayload represents the incoming webhook from Hyperswitch.
type HyperswitchWebhookPayload struct {
	EventType string                 `json:"event_type"`
	Content   map[string]any         `json:"content"`
}

// OrgSubscription holds the resolved subscription + plan for an org,
// used by the quota checker to enforce limits.
type OrgSubscription struct {
	Subscription *Subscription `json:"subscription,omitempty"`
	Plan         Plan          `json:"plan"`
}

// IsUnlimited returns true if the org is on the Enterprise tier (all limits are -1).
func (o *OrgSubscription) IsUnlimited() bool {
	return o.Plan.Tier == PlanTierEnterprise
}

// DefaultFreeSubscription returns the implicit subscription for orgs without
// an explicit subscription record (defaults to Free tier).
func DefaultFreeSubscription() OrgSubscription {
	plans := DefaultPlans()
	return OrgSubscription{Plan: plans[0]}
}

// UsageResponse is the response body for GET /billing/usage.
type UsageResponse struct {
	Plan                 Plan `json:"plan"`
	KBsUsed              int  `json:"kbs_used"`
	KBsLimit             int  `json:"kbs_limit"`
	SeatsUsed            int  `json:"seats_used"`
	SeatsLimit           int  `json:"seats_limit"`
	VoiceMinutesUsed     int  `json:"voice_minutes_used"`
	VoiceMinutesLimit    int  `json:"voice_minutes_limit"`
	ConcurrentVoiceUsed  int  `json:"concurrent_voice_used"`
	ConcurrentVoiceLimit int  `json:"concurrent_voice_limit"`
}

package model

import "time"

// SubscriptionStatus represents the lifecycle state of a subscription.
type SubscriptionStatus string

// SubscriptionStatusActive and related constants define the valid lifecycle states for a subscription.
const (
	SubscriptionStatusActive    SubscriptionStatus = "active"
	SubscriptionStatusCanceled  SubscriptionStatus = "canceled"
	SubscriptionStatusPastDue   SubscriptionStatus = "past_due"
	SubscriptionStatusTrialing  SubscriptionStatus = "trialing"
	SubscriptionStatusPaused    SubscriptionStatus = "paused"
	SubscriptionStatusExpired   SubscriptionStatus = "expired"
)

// PlanTier identifies a billing plan level.
type PlanTier string

// PlanTierFree and related constants define the available billing plan tiers.
const (
	PlanTierFree       PlanTier = "free"
	PlanTierPro        PlanTier = "pro"
	PlanTierEnterprise PlanTier = "enterprise"
)

// Plan describes a billing plan and its feature limits.
type Plan struct {
	ID                          string   `json:"id"`
	Tier                        PlanTier `json:"tier"`
	Name                        string   `json:"name"`
	PriceMonthly                int64    `json:"price_monthly"`  // amount in cents
	MaxUsers                    int      `json:"max_users"`
	MaxWorkspaces               int      `json:"max_workspaces"`
	MaxKBs                      int      `js…

…bs"`
	MaxStorageMB                int64    `json:"max_storage_mb"`
	MaxConcurrentVoiceSessions  int      `json:"max_concurrent_voice_sessions"` // -1 = unlimited
	MaxVoiceMinutesMonthly      int      `json:"max_voice_minutes_monthly"`     // -1 = unlimited
}

// DefaultPlans returns the pre-defined plans with their feature limits.
func DefaultPlans() []Plan {
	return []Plan{
		{
			ID:                         "plan_free",
			Tier:                       PlanTierFree,
			Name:                       "Free",
			PriceMonthly:               0,
			MaxUsers:                   5,
			MaxWorkspaces:              2,
			MaxKBs:                     3,
			MaxStorageMB:               500,
			MaxConcurrentVoiceSessions: 1,
			MaxVoiceMinutesMonthly:     60,
		},
		{
			ID:                         "plan_pro",
			Tier:                       PlanTierPro,
			Name:                       "Pro",
			PriceMonthly:               2900, // $29.00
			MaxUsers:                   25,
			MaxWorkspaces:              10,
			MaxKBs:                     50,
			MaxStorageMB:               10240,
			MaxConcurrentVoiceSessions: 5,
			MaxVoiceMinutesMonthly:     1200,
		},
		{
			ID:                         "plan_enterprise",
			Tier:                       PlanTierEnterprise,
			Name:                       "Enterprise",
			PriceMonthly:               9900, // $99.00
			MaxUsers:                   -1,   // unlimited
			MaxWorkspaces:              -1,
			MaxKBs:                     -1,
			MaxStorageMB:               -1,
			MaxConcurrentVoiceSessions: -1, // unlimited
			MaxVoiceMinutesMonthly:     -1,
		},
	}
}

// Check if org already has an active subscription.
	var existing *model.Subscription
	err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var e error
		existing, e = s.repo.GetActiveSubscription(ctx, tx, orgID)
		return e
	})
	if err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to check existing subscription", "error", err)
		return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to check existing subscription")
	}
	if existing != nil {
		return nil, apierror.NewConflict("organisation already has an active subscription: " + existing.ID)
	}

	// For the free plan, no payment orchestration is needed.
	if plan.Tier == model.PlanTierFree {
		sub := &model.Subscription{
			OrgID:              orgID,
			PlanID:             plan.ID,
			Status:             model.SubscriptionStatusActive,
			CurrentPeriodStart: now,
			CurrentPeriodEnd:   now.AddDate(0, 1, 0),
		}

		var result *model.Subscription
		err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
			var e error
			result, e = s.repo.CreateSubscription(ctx, tx, sub)
			return e
		})
		if err != nil {
			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to persist free subscription", "error", err)
			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to persist free subscription")
		}
		return result, nil
	}

	// Paid plan: create a payment via Hyperswitch with Razorpay as the connector.
	hsResp, err := s.hsClient.CreatePayment(ctx, &hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest{
		Amount:           plan.PriceMonthly,
		Currency:         "USD",
		CustomerID:       orgID,
		SetupFutureUsage: "off_session",
		Metadata: map[string]string{
			"plan_id": plan.ID,
			"org_id":  orgID,
		},
	})
	if err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to create Hyperswitch payment", "error", err)
		return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to create Hyperswitch payment")
	}

	sub := &model.Subscription{
		OrgID:                     orgID,
		PlanID:                    plan.ID,
		Status:                    model.SubscriptionStatusActive,
		HyperswitchSubscriptionID: hsResp.PaymentID,
		CurrentPeriodStart:        now,
		CurrentPeriodEnd:          now.AddDate(0, 1, 0),
	}

	var result *model.Subscription
	err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var e error
		result, e = s.repo.CreateSubscription(ctx, tx, sub)
		return e
	})
	if err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to persist subscription", "error", err)
		return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to persist subscription")
	}
	return result, nil
}

// CancelSubscription cancels the subscription for the given organisation.
func (s *BillingService) CancelSubscription(ctx context.Context, orgID string, subscriptionID string) error {
	var sub *model.Subscription
	err := db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var e error
		sub, e = s.repo.GetSubscriptionByID(ctx, tx, orgID, subscriptionID)
		return e
	})
	if err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to look up subscription", "error", err)
		return apierror.NewInternal("failed to look up subscription")
	}

### subscriptions migration
# subscriptions migration

-- +goose Up
-- Issue #50 — Hyperswitch payment orchestration: subscriptions table.
--
-- Tracks organisation billing subscriptions managed via Hyperswitch.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscriptions (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    org_id                      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    plan_id                     TEXT NOT NULL,
    status                      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active'
                                    CHECK (status IN ('active','canceled','past_due','trialing','paused','expired')),
    hyperswitch_subscription_id TEXT,
    current_period_start        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    current_period_end          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT (now() + interval '1 month'),
    created_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- One active subscription per organisation.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_subscriptions_org_active
    ON subscriptions (org_id)
    WHERE status IN ('active', 'trialing', 'past_due');

-- Look-up by Hyperswitch ID for webhook processing.
CREATE INDEX idx_subscriptions_hyperswitch_id
    ON subscriptions (hyperswitch_subscription_id)
    WHERE hyperswitch_subscription_id IS NOT NULL;

-- +goose Down
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_subscriptions_hyperswitch_id;
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_subscriptions_org_active;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS subscriptions;

			return fmt.Errorf("extend subscription period: %w", err)
		}
	}

	return tx.Commit(ctx)
}

// CreatePaymentIntent creates a one-off payment intent via Hyperswitch.
func (s *BillingService) CreatePaymentIntent(ctx context.Context, orgID string, req model.CreatePaymentIntentRequest) (*model.PaymentIntent, error) {
	hsResp, err := s.hsClient.CreatePayment(ctx, &hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest{
		Amount:     req.Amount,
		Currency:   req.Currency,
		CustomerID: orgID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to create Hyperswitch payment", "error", err)
		return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to create Hyperswitch payment")
	}

	pi := &model.PaymentIntent{
		OrgID:                orgID,
		Amount:               req.Amount,
		Currency:             req.Currency,
		Status:               model.PaymentIntentStatusRequiresPayment,
		HyperswitchPaymentID: hsResp.PaymentID,
		ClientSecret:         hsResp.ClientSecret,
	}

	var result *model.PaymentIntent
	if err := db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var e error
		result, e = s.repo.CreatePaymentIntent(ctx, tx, pi)
		return e
	}); err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to persist payment intent", "error", err)
		// Best-effort cancel the orphaned Hyperswitch payment with a fresh
		// context — the original ctx may already be canceled/timed out.
		cleanupCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		if cancelErr := s.hsClient.CancelPayment(cleanupCtx, hsResp.PaymentID); cancelErr != nil {
			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to cancel orphaned Hyperswitch payment", "payment_id", hsResp.PaymentID, "error", cancelErr)
		}
		return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to persist payment intent")
	}

	return result, nil
}

// VerifyWebhookSignature verifies the Hyperswitch webhook HMAC-SHA256 signature.
func (s *BillingService) VerifyWebhookSignature(payload []byte, signature string) error {
	if s.webhookSecret == "" {
		slog.Warn("webhook signature verification skipped: RAVEN_HYPERSWITCH_WEBHOOK_SECRET is not configured")
		return apierror.NewUnauthorized("webhook signature verification is not configured")
	}

	mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, []byte(s.webhookSecret))
	mac.Write(payload)
	expected := hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil))

	if !hmac.Equal([]byte(expected), []byte(signature)) {
		return apierror.NewUnauthorized("invalid webhook signature")
	}
	return nil
}

// HandleWebhook processes a verified Hyperswitch webhook event.
// Events are idempotent: replaying the same event is a no-op.
func (s *BillingService) HandleWebhook(ctx context.Context, event model.HyperswitchWebhookPayload) error {
	paymentID, _ := event.Content["payment_id"].(string)
	if paymentID == "" {
		slog.Warn("webhook event missing payment_id", "event_type", event.EventType)
		return nil
	}

	// Extract org_id from metadata if available.
	orgID := extractOrgIDFromWebhook(event)

	switch event.EventType {
	case "payment_succeeded":
		return s.handlePaymentSucceeded(ctx, paymentID, orgID, event)
	case "payment_failed":
		return s.handlePaymentFailed

// Webhook handles POST /api/v1/billing/webhook.
// This endpoint is called by Hyperswitch to notify about payment events.
// It does NOT require JWT authentication; it uses webhook signature verification.
//
// @Summary     Hyperswitch webhook
// @Tags        billing
// @Accept      json
// @Produce     json
// @Param       X-Webhook-Signature header string true "HMAC-SHA256 signature"
// @Success     200 {object} map[string]string
// @Failure     401 {object} apierror.AppError
// @Failure     400 {object} apierror.AppError
// @Router      /billing/webhook [post]
func (h *BillingHandler) Webhook(c *gin.Context) {
	const maxWebhookBody = 1 << 20 // 1 MB
	payload, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(c.Request.Body, maxWebhookBody+1))
	if err != nil {
		c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusBadRequest, apierror.AppError{
			Code:    http.StatusBadRequest,
			Message: "Bad Request",
			Detail:  "failed to read request body",
		})
		return
	}
	if len(payload) > maxWebhookBody {
		c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, apierror.AppError{
			Code:    http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge,
			Message: "Payload Too Large",
			Detail:  "webhook body exceeds 1 MB limit",
		})
		return
	}

	signature := c.GetHeader("X-Webhook-Signature")
	if err := h.svc.VerifyWebhookSignature(payload, signature); err != nil {
		_ = c.Error(err)
		c.Abort()
		return
	}

> AGENT

Good picture of what exists. The core billing plumbing is in place (Hyperswitch, webhook, quota), but the pricing model we designed is significantly different from what's implemented. Key gaps:

- Plans are **flat-rate USD** ($29/$99), not **per-seat INR**
- No **annual billing** or 20% discount
- No **trial lifecycle** (status exists, no automation)
- No **minimum seat enforcement**
- No **proration**, **refunds**, or **data retention pipeline**
- No **Enterprise manual onboarding** flow
- No **self-serve checkout UI**

Here are the proposed vertical slices:

---

**1. Per-seat pricing model** `HITL`
Migrate Plan model from flat-rate USD to per-seat INR, add `min_seats` and `seat_count` to subscriptions, update quota enforcement.
**Blocked by:** None — foundational, blocks everything else.

**2. Annual billing & 20% discount** `AFK`
Add `billing_cycle` (monthly/annual) to subscriptions, annual plan price = 10× monthly, update period calculation and Hyperswitch payment amount.
**Blocked by:** #1

**3. 14-day trial lifecycle & grace period** `AFK`
Add `trial_ends_at` / `grace_period_ends_at` columns, Asynq daily job: lock on day 15, archive on day 22, delete on day 52. Email notifications at day 12, 15, 21.
**Blocked by:** #1

**4. Mid-cycle seat proration** `AFK`
`PATCH /billing/subscriptions/:id/seats` endpoint: calculate days remaining, create immediate Hyperswitch payment intent for prorated amount.
**Blocked by:** #1, #2

**5. Enterprise manual onboarding** `HITL`
Admin-only endpoint to create Enterprise subscription (bypass checkout), annual-only enforcement, custom per-seat price override, Slack notification to founder on signup.
**Blocked by:** #1

**6. Prorated annual refund on cancellation** `AFK`
On cancellation of annual subscription, calculate unused months and trigger Hyperswitch refund.
**Blocked by:** #2

**7. Self-serve Pro checkout UI** `AFK`
Vue.js page: seat selector (min 5), monthly/annual toggle (shows 20% saving), Hyperswitch SDK for payment, success/failure states.
**Blocked by:** #1, #2

---

Does the granularity feel right? Any slices to merge or split? Are the HITL/AFK labels correct?

A new subscription starts in \\`trialing\\` status with \\`trial_ends_at = created_at + 14 days\\`. The job transitions state and triggers emails at fixed points:\n\n| Day | Action |\n|-----|--------|\n| 12 | Warning email: \"trial ends in 2 days\" |\n| 15 | Lock: status → \\`expired\\`, feature access blocked |\n| 21 | Final warning email: \"data deleted in 1 day\" |\n| 22 | Archive: workspaces/KBs compressed to cold storage |\n| 52 | Delete: org data permanently removed |\n\nThe two timestamps are stored on \\`subscriptions\\`: \\`trial_ends_at\\` and \\`grace_period_ends_at\\` (= trial_ends_at + 7 days). Email sending uses the existing notification infrastructure. A reactivated subscription (payment received during grace period) must cancel pending delete jobs.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Migration adds `trial_ends_at TIMESTAMPTZ` and `grace_period_ends_at TIMESTAMPTZ` to `subscriptions`\n- [ ] New subscriptions default to `trialing` status with `trial_ends_at = now() + 14 days`\n- [ ] Daily Asynq job transitions status and enqueues email tasks at correct days\n- [ ] Feature-access middleware returns 402 when `status = expired`\n- [ ] Payment during grace period reactivates subscription and cancels pending delete job\n- [ ] Archive and delete jobs are idempotent (safe to re-run)\n- [ ] Tests cover each day-boundary transition\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #591\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -1",
  "description": "Create issue 3: trial lifecycle"
}

New subscriptions default to `trialing` status with `trial_ends_at = now() + 14 days`, `grace_period_ends_at = now() + 21 days`\n   - Add `ReactivateSubscription(ctx, orgID, subscriptionID)` method — sets status back to `active`, clears trial timestamps, cancels pending delete jobs\n\n4. **Daily Asynq job** (`internal/jobs/trial_lifecycle.go`):\n   - Day 21 (grace_period_ends_at): enqueue email task `data_deletion_warning`\n   - Day 22: enqueue `archive_org_data` task (mark org as archived)\n   - Day 52 (22 + 30): enqueue `delete_org_data` task\n\n5. **Middleware**: in the quota/feature-access middleware, return HTTP 402 with `{\"error\": \"subscription_expired\"}` when org subscription status is `expired`.\n\n6. **Job registration** (`internal/jobs/tasks.go`): register the new `trial_lifecycle` periodic task to run daily.\n\n7. **Tests**: cover each day-boundary transition (day 12, 15, 21, 22, 52) and the 402 middleware behavior.\n\n**Look at existing jobs** (`internal/jobs/usage.go`, `internal/jobs/voice_usage.go`) for patterns on how Asynq jobs are structured in this project.\n\n**Rules:**\n- Every commit signed off: `git commit -s`\n- Run `golangci-lint run --new-from-rev=HEAD ./...` — fix new violations\n- No `Co-Authored-By` trailers\n- Do NOT push or create a PR\n\nWhen done, output the worktree path and branch name."
}

1	package service

3	import (
4		"context"
5		"crypto/hmac"

7		"encoding/hex"

16		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/db"

19		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pkg/apierror"
20	)
21	
22	// BillingRepository defines the persistence interface for billing operations.
23	type BillingRepository interface {
24		CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, sub *model.Subscription) (*model.Subscription, error)
25		GetSubscriptionByID(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, orgID, subscriptionID string) (*model.Subscription, error)
26		GetSubscriptionByHyperswitchID(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, hsID string) (*model.Subscription, error)
27		GetActiveSubscription(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, orgID string) (*model.Subscription, error)
28		UpdateSubscriptionStatus(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, orgID, subscriptionID string, status model.SubscriptionStatus) (*model.Subscription, error)
29		ExtendSubscriptionPeriod(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, hyperswitchID string) (*model.Subscription, error)
30		CreatePaymentIntent(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, pi *model.PaymentIntent) (*model.PaymentIntent, error)
31		InsertPaymentEvent(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, orgID, eventType, paymentID, status string, rawPayload []byte) (bool, error)
32	}
33	
34	// HyperswitchClient defines the interface for Hyperswitch API operations.
35	type HyperswitchClient interface {
36		CreatePayment(ctx context.Context, req *hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest) (*hyperswitch.PaymentResponse, error)
37		CancelPayment(ctx context.Context, paymentID string) error
38	}
39	
40	// BillingService contains business logic for subscription and payment management
41	// via the Hyperswitch payment orchestration platform.
42	type BillingService struct {
43		repo          BillingRepository
44		pool          *pgxpool.Pool
45		hsClient      HyperswitchClient
46		webhookSecret string
47		plans         map[string]model.Plan
48	}
49	
50	// NewBillingService creates a new BillingService.
51	func NewBillingService(repo BillingRepository, pool *pgxpool.Pool, hsClient HyperswitchClient, webhookSecret string) *BillingService {
52		plans := make(map[string]model.Plan)
53		for _, p := range model.DefaultPlans() {
54			plans[p.ID] = p
55		}
56		return &BillingService{
57			repo:          repo,
58			pool:          pool,
59			hsClient:      hsClient,
60			webhookSecret: webhookSecret,
61			plans:         plans,
62		}
63	}
64	
65	// GetPlans returns the available billing plans.
66	func (s *BillingService) GetPlans() []model.Plan {
67		return model.DefaultPlans()
68	}
69	
70	// GetPlanByID returns a plan by ID, or an error if not found.
71	func (s *BillingService) GetPlanByID(planID string) (*model.Plan, error) {
72		p, ok := s.plans[planID]
73		if !ok {
74			return nil, apierror.NewNotFound("plan not found: " + planID)
75		}
76		return &p, nil
77	}
78	
79	// GetActiveSubscription returns the current active subscription for an org, or nil.
80	func (s *BillingService) GetActiveSubscription(ctx context.Context, orgID string) (*model.Subscription, error) {
81		var sub *model.Subscription
82		err := db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
83			var e error
84			sub, e = s.repo.GetActiveSubscription(ctx, tx, orgID)
85			return e
86		})
87		if err != nil {
88			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to retrieve subscription")
89		}
90		return sub, nil
91	}
92	
93	// CreateSubscription creates a new subscription for the given organisation.
94	// For paid plans, it calls Hyperswitch to set up a recurring payment via Razorpay.
95	func (s *BillingService) CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, orgID string, req model.CreateSubscriptionRequest) (*model.Subscription, error) {
96		plan, err := s.GetPlanByID(req.PlanID)
97		if err != nil {
98			return nil, err
99		}
100	
101		now := time.Now().UTC()
102	
103		// Check if org already has an active subscription.
104		var existing *model.Subscription
105		err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
106			var e error
107			existing, e = s.repo.GetActiveSubscription(ctx, tx, orgID)
108			return e
109		})
110		if err != nil {
111			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to check existing subscription", "error", err)
112			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to check existing subscription")
113		}
114		if existing != nil {
115			return nil, apierror.NewConflict("organisation already has an active subscription: " + existing.ID)
116		}
117	
118		// For the free plan, no payment orchestration is needed.
119		if plan.Tier == model.PlanTierFree {
120			sub := &model.Subscription{
121				OrgID:              orgID,
122				PlanID:             plan.ID,
123				Status:             model.SubscriptionStatusActive,
124				CurrentPeriodStart: now,
125				CurrentPeriodEnd:   now.AddDate(0, 1, 0),
126			}
127	
128			var result *model.Subscription
129			err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
130				var e error
131				result, e = s.repo.CreateSubscription(ctx, tx, sub)
132				return e
133			})
134			if err != nil {
135				slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to persist free subscription", "error", err)
136				return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to persist free subscription")
137			}
138			return result, nil
139		}
140	
141		// Paid plan: create a payment via Hyperswitch with Razorpay as the connector.
142		hsResp, err := s.hsClient.CreatePayment(ctx, &hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest{
143			Amount:           plan.PriceMonthly,
144			Currency:         "USD",
145			CustomerID:       orgID,
146			SetupFutureUsage: "off_session",
147			Metadata: map[string]string{
148				"plan_id": plan.ID,
149				"org_id":  orgID,
150			},
151		})
152		if err != nil {
153			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to create Hyperswitch payment", "error", err)
154			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to create Hyperswitch payment")
155		}
156	
157		sub := &model.Subscription{
158			OrgID:                     orgID,
159			PlanID:                    plan.ID,
160			Status:                    model.SubscriptionStatusActive,
161			HyperswitchSubscriptionID: hsResp.PaymentID,
162			CurrentPeriodStart:        now,
163			CurrentPeriodEnd:          now.AddDate(0, 1, 0),
164		}
165	
166		var result *model.Subscription
167		err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
168			var e error
169			result, e = s.repo.CreateSubscription(ctx, tx, sub)
170			return e
171		})
172		if err != nil {
173			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to persist subscription", "error", err)
174			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to persist subscription")
175		}
176		return result, nil
177	}
178	
179	// CancelSubscription cancels the subscription for the given organisation.
180	func (s *BillingService) CancelSubscription(ctx context.Context, orgID string, subscriptionID string) error {
181		var sub *model.Subscription
182		err := db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
183			var e error
184			sub, e = s.repo.GetSubscriptionByID(ctx, tx, orgID, subscriptionID)
185			return e
186		})
187		if err != nil {
188			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to look up subscription", "error", err)
189			return apierror.NewInternal("failed to look up subscription")
190		}
191		if sub == nil {
192			return apierror.NewNotFound("subscription not found")
193		}
194	
195		// Cancel on Hyperswitch if there is a linked payment.
196		if sub.HyperswitchSubscriptionID != "" {
197			if err := s.hsClient.CancelPayment(ctx, sub.HyperswitchSubscriptionID); err != nil {
198				slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to cancel Hyperswitch payment", "error", err)
199				return apierror.NewInternal("failed to cancel Hyperswitch payment")
200			}
201		}
202	
203		// Update status in DB.
204		return db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
205			_, e := s.repo.UpdateSubscriptionStatus(ctx, tx, orgID, subscriptionID, model.SubscriptionStatusCanceled)
206			return e
207		})
208	}
209	
210	// CreatePaymentIntent creates a one-off payment intent via Hyperswitch.
211	func (s *BillingService) CreatePaymentIntent(ctx context.Context, orgID string, req model.CreatePaymentIntentRequest) (*model.PaymentIntent, error) {
212		hsResp, err := s.hsClient.CreatePayment(ctx, &hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest{
213			Amount:     req.Amount,
214			Currency:   req.Currency,
215			CustomerID: orgID,
216		})
217		if err != nil {
218			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to create Hyperswitch payment", "error", err)
219			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to create Hyperswitch payment")
220		}
221	
222		pi := &model.PaymentIntent{
223			OrgID:                orgID,
224			Amount:               req.Amount,
225			Currency:             req.Currency,
226			Status:               model.PaymentIntentStatusRequiresPayment,
227			HyperswitchPaymentID: hsResp.PaymentID,
228			ClientSecret:         hsResp.ClientSecret,
229		}
230	
231		var result *model.PaymentIntent
232		if err := db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
233			var e error
234			result, e = s.repo.CreatePaymentIntent(ctx, tx, pi)
235			return e
236		}); err != nil {
237			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to persist payment intent", "error", err)
238			// Best-effort cancel the orphaned Hyperswitch payment with a fresh
239			// context — the original ctx may already be canceled/timed out.
240			cleanupCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
241			defer cancel()
242			if cancelErr := s.hsClient.CancelPayment(cleanupCtx, hsResp.PaymentID); cancelErr != nil {
243				slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "failed to cancel orphaned Hyperswitch payment", "payment_id", hsResp.PaymentID, "error", cancelErr)
244			}
245			return nil, apierror.NewInternal("failed to persist payment intent")
246		}
247	
248		return result, nil
249	}
250	
251	// VerifyWebhookSignature verifies the Hyperswitch webhook HMAC-SHA256 signature.
252	func (s *BillingService) VerifyWebhookSignature(payload []byte, signature string) error {
253		if s.webhookSecret == "" {
254			slog.Warn("webhook signature verification skipped: RAVEN_HYPERSWITCH_WEBHOOK_SECRET is not configured")
255			return apierror.NewUnauthorized("webhook signature verification is not configured")
256		}
257	
258		mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, []byte(s.webhookSecret))
259		mac.Write(payload)
260		expected := hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil))
261	
262		if !hmac.Equal([]byte(expected), []byte(signature)) {
263			return apierror.NewUnauthorized("invalid webhook signature")
264		}
265		return nil
266	}
267	
268	// HandleWebhook processes a verified Hyperswitch webhook event.
269	// Events are idempotent: replaying the same event is a no-op.
270	func (s *BillingService) HandleWebhook(ctx context.Context, event model.HyperswitchWebhookPayload) error {
271		paymentID, _ := event.Content["payment_id"].(string)
272		if paymentID == "" {
273			slog.Warn("webhook event missing payment_id", "event_type", event.EventType)
274			return nil
275		}
276	
277		// Extract org_id from metadata if available.
278		orgID := extractOrgIDFromWebhook(event)
279	
280		switch event.EventType {
281		case "payment_succeeded":
282			return s.handlePaymentSucceeded(ctx, paymentID, orgID, event)
283		case "payment_failed":
284			return s.handlePaymentFailed(ctx, paymentID, orgID, event)
285		case "subscription_cancelled":
286			return s.handleSubscriptionCancelled(ctx, paymentID, orgID, event)
287		default:
288			slog.Info("unhandled webhook event type", "event_type", event.EventType)
289			return nil
290		}
291	}
292	
293	func (s *BillingService) handlePaymentSucceeded(ctx context.Context, paymentID, orgID string, event model.HyperswitchWebhookPayload) error {
294		rawPayload, _ := json.Marshal(event)
295	
296		// Use a bypass-RLS transaction for webhook processing since we may not
297		// have org context from the webhook caller.
298		tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
299		if err != nil {
300			return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
301		}
302		defer tx.Rollback(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
303	
304		// Enable RLS bypass for webhook processing.
305		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "SELECT set_config('app.bypass_rls', 'true', true)"); err != nil {
306			return fmt.Errorf("set bypass_rls: %w", err)
307		}
308	
309		// Idempotency check.
310		isNew, err := s.repo.InsertPaymentEvent(ctx, tx, orgID, event.EventType, paymentID, "succeeded", rawPayload)
311		if err != nil {
312			return fmt.Errorf("insert payment event: %w", err)
313		}
314		if !isNew {
315			slog.Info("duplicate webhook event, skipping", "payment_id", paymentID, "event_type", event.EventType)
316			return tx.Commit(ctx)
317		}
318	
319		// Find subscription by Hyperswitch ID and extend billing period.
320		sub, err := s.repo.GetSubscriptionByHyperswitchID(ctx, tx, paymentID)
321		if err != nil {
322			return fmt.Errorf("get subscription by hyperswitch id: %w", err)
323		}
324		if sub != nil {
325			if _, err := s.repo.ExtendSubscriptionPeriod(ctx, tx, paymentID); err != nil {
326				return fmt.Errorf("extend subscription period: %w", err)
327			}
328		}
329	
330		return tx.Commit(ctx)
331	}
332	
333	func (s *BillingService) handlePaymentFailed(ctx context.Context, paymentID, orgID string, event model.HyperswitchWebhookPayload) error {
334		rawPayload, _ := json.Marshal(event)
335	
336		tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
337		if err != nil {
338			return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
339		}
340		defer tx.Rollback(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
341	
342		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "SELECT set_config('app.bypass_rls', 'true', true)"); err != nil {
343			return fmt.Errorf("set bypass_rls: %w", err)
344		}
345	
346		isNew, err := s.repo.InsertPaymentEvent(ctx, tx, orgID, event.EventType, paymentID, "failed", rawPayload)
347		if err != nil {
348			return fmt.Errorf("insert payment event: %w", err)
349		}
350		if !isNew {
351			return tx.Commit(ctx)
352		}
353	
354		// Mark subscription as past_due — triggers free tier downgrade.
355		sub, err := s.repo.GetSubscriptionByHyperswitchID(ctx, tx, paymentID)
356		if err != nil {
357			return fmt.Errorf("get subscription by hyperswitch id: %w", err)
358		}
359		if sub != nil {
360			if _, err := s.repo.UpdateSubscriptionStatus(ctx, tx, sub.OrgID, sub.ID, model.SubscriptionStatusPastDue); err != nil {
361				return fmt.Errorf("update subscription status: %w", err)
362			}
363		}
364	
365		return tx.Commit(ctx)
366	}
367	
368	func (s *BillingService) handleSubscriptionCancelled(ctx context.Context, paymentID, orgID string, event model.HyperswitchWebhookPayload) error {
369		rawPayload, _ := json.Marshal(event)
370	
371		tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
372		if err != nil {
373			return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
374		}
375		defer tx.Rollback(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
376	
377		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "SELECT set_config('app.bypass_rls', 'true', true)"); err != nil {
378			return fmt.Errorf("set bypass_rls: %w", err)
379		}
380	
381		isNew, err := s.repo.InsertPaymentEvent(ctx, tx, orgID, event.EventType, paymentID, "cancelled", rawPayload)
382		if err != nil {
383			return fmt.Errorf("insert payment event: %w", err)
384		}
385		if !isNew {
386			return tx.Commit(ctx)
387		}
388	
389		sub, err := s.repo.GetSubscriptionByHyperswitchID(ctx, tx, paymentID)
390		if err != nil {
391			return fmt.Errorf("get subscription by hyperswitch id: %w", err)
392		}
393		if sub != nil {
394			if _, err := s.repo.UpdateSubscriptionStatus(ctx, tx, sub.OrgID, sub.ID, model.SubscriptionStatusCanceled); err != nil {
395				return fmt.Errorf("update subscription status: %w", err)
396			}
397		}
398	
399		return tx.Commit(ctx)
400	}
401	
402	// extractOrgIDFromWebhook attempts to extract org_id from the webhook payload metadata.
403	func extractOrgIDFromWebhook(event model.HyperswitchWebhookPayload) string {
404		metadata, ok := event.Content["metadata"].(map[string]any)
405		if !ok {
406			return ""
407		}
408		orgID, _ := metadata["org_id"].(string)
409		return orgID
410	}
411

  "new_string": "// CreateSubscription creates a new subscription for the given organisation.\n// For paid plans, it calls Hyperswitch to set up a recurring payment via Razorpay.\nfunc (s *BillingService) CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, orgID string, req model.CreateSubscriptionRequest) (*model.Subscription, error) {\n\tplan, err := s.GetPlanByID(req.PlanID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tif plan.MinSeats > 0 && req.SeatCount < plan.MinSeats {\n\t\treturn nil, apierror.NewUnprocessable(fmt.Sprintf(\"plan %q requires a minimum of %d seats\", plan.ID, plan.MinSeats))\n\t}\n\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\n\t// Check if org already has an active subscription.\n\tvar existing *model.Subscription\n\terr = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {\n\t\tvar e error\n\t\texisting, e = s.repo.GetActiveSubscription(ctx, tx, orgID)\n\t\treturn e\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tslog.ErrorContext(ctx, \"failed to check existing subscription\", \"error\", err)\n\t\treturn nil, apierror.NewInternal(\"failed to check existing subscription\")\n\t}\n\tif existing != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, apierror.NewConflict(\"organisation already has an active subscription: \" + existing.ID)\n\t}\n\n\t// For the free plan, no payment orchestration is needed.\n\tif plan.Tier == model.PlanTierFree {\n\t\tsub := &model.Subscription{\n\t\t\tOrgID:              orgID,\n\t\t\tPlanID:             plan.ID,\n\t\t\tStatus:             model.SubscriptionStatusActive,\n\t\t\tSeatCount:          req.SeatCount,\n\t\t\tCurrentPeriodStart: now,\n\t\t\tCurrentPeriodEnd:   now.AddDate(0, 1, 0),\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar result *model.Subscription\n\t\terr = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {\n\t\t\tvar e error\n\t\t\tresult, e = s.repo.CreateSubscription(ctx, tx, sub)\n\t\t\treturn e\n\t\t})\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tslog.ErrorContext(ctx, \"failed to persist free subscription\", \"error\", err)\n\t\t\treturn nil, apierror.NewInternal(\"failed to persist free subscription\")\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Paid plan: charge per seat in INR via Hyperswitch with Razorpay as the connector.\n\tamount := plan.PricePerSeatMonthly * int64(req.SeatCount)\n\thsResp, err := s.hsClient.CreatePayment(ctx, &hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest{\n\t\tAmount:           amount,\n\t\tCurrency:         \"INR\",\n\t\tCustomerID:       orgID,\n\t\tSetupFutureUsage: \"off_session\",\n\t\tMetadata: map[string]string{\n\t\t\t\"plan_id\":    plan.ID,\n\t\t\t\"org_id\":     orgID,\n\t\t\t\"seat_count\": fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", req.SeatCount),\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tslog.ErrorContext(ctx, \"failed to create Hyperswitch payment\", \"error\", err)\n\t\treturn nil, apierror.NewInternal(\"failed to create Hyperswitch payment\")\n\t}\n\n\tsub := &model.Subscription{\n\t\tOrgID:                     orgID,\n\t\tPlanID:                    plan.ID,\n\t\tStatus:                    model.SubscriptionStatusActive,\n\t\tSeatCount:                 req.SeatCount,\n\t\tHyperswitchSubscriptionID: hsResp.PaymentID,\n\t\tCurrentPeriodStart:        now,\n\t\tCurrentPeriodEnd:          now.AddDate(0, 1, 0),\n\t}\n\n\tvar result *model.Subscription\n\terr = db.WithOrgID(ctx, s.pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {\n\t\tvar e error\n\t\tresult, e = s.repo.CreateSubscription(ctx, tx, sub)\n\t\treturn e\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tslog.ErrorContext(ctx, \"failed to persist subscription\", \"error\", err)\n\t\treturn nil, apierror.NewInternal(\"failed to persist subscription\")\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}"
}

1	package repository
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"errors"
6		"fmt"
7		"time"
8	
9		"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
10		"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
11	
12		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/model"
13	)
14	
15	// BillingRepository handles database operations for subscriptions, payment events,
16	// and usage queries. All operations run inside a pgx.Tx with org_id set for RLS.
17	type BillingRepository struct {
18		pool *pgxpool.Pool
19	}
20	
21	// NewBillingRepository creates a new BillingRepository.
22	func NewBillingRepository(pool *pgxpool.Pool) *BillingRepository {
23		return &BillingRepository{pool: pool}
24	}
25	
26	const (
27		sqlSubscriptionInsert = `
28			INSERT INTO subscriptions (org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id, current_period_start, current_period_end)
29			VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
30			RETURNING id, org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id,
31			          current_period_start, current_period_end, created_at`
32	
33		sqlSubscriptionByID = `
34			SELECT id, org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id,
35			       current_period_start, current_period_end, created_at
36			FROM subscriptions
37			WHERE id = $1 AND org_id = $2`
38	
39		sqlSubscriptionByHyperswitchID = `
40			SELECT id, org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id,
41			       current_period_start, current_period_end, created_at
42			FROM subscriptions
43			WHERE hyperswitch_subscription_id = $1`
44	
45		sqlSubscriptionActiveByOrg = `
46			SELECT id, org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id,
47			       current_period_start, current_period_end, created_at
48			FROM subscriptions
49			WHERE org_id = $1 AND status IN ('active', 'trialing', 'past_due')
50			LIMIT 1`
51	
52		sqlSubscriptionUpdateStatus = `
53			UPDATE subscriptions SET status = $3
54			WHERE id = $1 AND org_id = $2
55			RETURNING id, org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id,
56			          current_period_start, current_period_end, created_at`
57	
58		sqlSubscriptionExtendPeriod = `
59			UPDATE subscriptions SET
60				status = 'active',
61				current_period_start = now(),
62				current_period_end   = now() + interval '1 month'
63			WHERE hyperswitch_subscription_id = $1
64			RETURNING id, org_id, plan_id, status, hyperswitch_subscription_id,
65			          current_period_start, current_period_end, created_at`
66	
67		sqlPaymentIntentInsert = `
68			INSERT INTO payment_intents (org_id, amount, currency, status, hyperswitch_payment_id, client_secret)
69			VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
70			RETURNING id, org_id, amount, currency, status, hyperswitch_payment_id, client_secret, created_at`
71	
72		sqlPaymentEventInsert = `
73			INSERT INTO payment_events (org_id, event_type, payment_id, status, raw_payload)
74			VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
75			ON CONFLICT (payment_id, event_type) DO NOTHING
76			RETURNING id`
77	
78		sqlPaymentEventExists = `
79			SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM payment_events WHERE payment_id = $1 AND event_type = $2)`
80	
81		sqlCountKBsByOrg = `
82			SELECT COUNT(*) FROM knowledge_bases
83			WHERE org_id = $1 AND archived_at IS NULL`
84	
85		sqlCountMembersByOrg = `
86			SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) FROM workspace_members
87			WHERE workspace_id IN (SELECT id FROM workspaces WHERE org_id = $1)`
88	
89		sqlVoiceUsageForPeriod = `
90			SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_duration_seconds), 0)
91			FROM voice_usage_summaries
92			WHERE org_id = $1 AND period_start >= $2`
93	)
94	
95	func scanSubscription(row pgx.Row) (*model.Subscription, error) {
96		var s model.Subscription
97		err := row.Scan(
98			&s.ID,
99			&s.OrgID,
100			&s.PlanID,
101			&s.Status,
102			&s.HyperswitchSubscriptionID,
103			&s.CurrentPeriodStart,
104			&s.CurrentPeriodEnd,
105			&s.CreatedAt,
106		)
107		if err != nil {
108			return nil, err
109		}
110		return &s, nil
111	}
112	
113	// CreateSubscription inserts a new subscription and returns it with DB-assigned fields.
114	func (r *BillingRepository) CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, sub *model.Subscription) (*model.Subscription, error) {
115		row := tx.QueryRow(ctx, sqlSubscriptionInsert,
116			sub.OrgID,
117			sub.PlanID,
118			sub.Status,
119			sub.HyperswitchSubscriptionID,
120			sub.CurrentPeriodStart,

74	// TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_TooFewSeats verifies that SeatCount < 20 is rejected.
75	func TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_TooFewSeats(t *testing.T) {
76		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, "", "")
77	
78		req := model.CreateEnterpriseSubscriptionRequest{
79			OrgID:         "org_test",
80			SeatCount:     10, // below the 20-seat minimum
81			ContractStart: time.Now().UTC(),
82			ContractEnd:   time.Now().UTC().AddDate(1, 0, 0),
83		}
84	
85		_, err := svc.CreateEnterpriseSubscription(context.Background(), req)
86		require.Error(t, err)
87		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "20")
88	}
89	
90	// TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_BoundarySeats verifies the 20-seat minimum boundary.
91	func TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_BoundarySeats(t *testing.T) {
92		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, "", "")
93	
94		for _, tc := range []struct {
95			seats   int
96			wantErr bool
97		}{
98			{seats: 1, wantErr: true},
99			{seats: 19, wantErr: true},
100		} {
101			req := model.CreateEnterpriseSubscriptionRequest{
102				OrgID:         "org_test",
103				SeatCount:     tc.seats,
104				ContractStart: time.Now().UTC(),
105				ContractEnd:   time.Now().UTC().AddDate(1, 0, 0),
106			}
107			_, err := svc.CreateEnterpriseSubscription(context.Background(), req)
108			require.Error(t, err, "expected seat-count error for seats=%d", tc.seats)
109			assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "20", "error must mention minimum seat count")
110		}
111	}
112	
113	// TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_NoHyperswitchCall verifies Hyperswitch is never called
114	// for enterprise subscriptions -- invoicing is external.
115	func TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_NoHyperswitchCall(t *testing.T) {
116		hsClient := &trackingHSClient{}
117		// Use seat count below minimum so we get a validation error before any external call.
118		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, hsClient, "", "")
119	
120		req := model.CreateEnterpriseSubscriptionRequest{
121			OrgID:         "org_test",
122			SeatCount:     5, // below minimum -- validation exits before DB/HS
123			ContractStart: time.Now().UTC(),
124			ContractEnd:   time.Now().UTC().AddDate(1, 0, 0),
125		}
126	
127		_, err := svc.CreateEnterpriseSubscription(context.Background(), req)
128		require.Error(t, err)

130	}

132	// TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_SlackNotFiredWhenDBFails verifies that Slack is only
133	// sent after a successful DB write. When the DB fails the goroutine must not be spawned.
134	func TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_SlackNotFiredWhenDBFails(t *testing.T) {
135		slackCh := make(chan struct{}, 1)
136		srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
137			slackCh <- struct{}{}
138			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
139		}))
140		defer srv.Close()
141	
142		// Service with Slack URL set but nil pool -> DB error -> Slack goroutine not launched.
143		svc := service.NewBillingService(&mockBillingRepo{}, nil, nil, "", srv.URL)
144	
145		req := model.CreateEnterpriseSubscriptionRequest{
146			OrgID:         "org_slack",
147			SeatCount:     30,
148			ContractStart: time.Now().UTC(),
149			ContractEnd:   time.Now().UTC().AddDate(1, 0, 0),
150		}
151	
152		_, err := svc.CreateEnterpriseSubscription(context.Background(), req)
153		assert.Error(t, err, "expected error due to nil pool")
154	
155		select {
156		case <-slackCh:
157			t.Error("Slack must NOT be called when DB write fails")
158		case <-time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):
159			// expected: no Slack call
160		}
161	}
162	
163	// TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_EmptySlackURL_NoCall verifies that when slackWebhookURL
164	// is empty no HTTP request is sent to Slack, even after a seat-count validation failure.
165	func TestCreateEnterpriseSubscription_EmptySlackURL_NoCall(t *testing.T) {
166		slackCalled := false
167		srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
168			slackCalled = true
169			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
170		}))
171		defer srv.Close()
172	
173		// slackWebhookURL="" -- Slack must never be called.

175	
176		req := model.CreateEnterpriseSubscriptionRequest{

183		_, err := svc.CreateEnterpriseSubscription(context.Background(), req)

185

188	}

45	func TestVerifyWebhookSignature_ValidSignature(t *testing.T) {
46		secret := "test-webhook-secret-key"
47		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, secret, "")
48	
49		payload := []byte(`{"event_type":"payment_succeeded","content":{"payment_id":"pay_123"}}`)
50		mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, []byte(secret))
51		mac.Write(payload)
52		validSig := hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil))
53	
54		err := svc.VerifyWebhookSignature(payload, validSig)
55		assert.NoError(t, err)
56	}
57	
58	func TestVerifyWebhookSignature_InvalidSignature(t *testing.T) {
59		secret := "test-webhook-secret-key"
60		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, secret, "")
61	
62		payload := []byte(`{"event_type":"payment_succeeded"}`)
63		err := svc.VerifyWebhookSignature(payload, "bad_signature")
64		require.Error(t, err)
65		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "invalid webhook signature")
66	}
67	
68	func TestVerifyWebhookSignature_EmptySecret_Rejects(t *testing.T) {
69		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, "", "")
70	
71		payload := []byte(`{"event_type":"payment_succeeded"}`)
72		err := svc.VerifyWebhookSignature(payload, "any_signature")
73		require.Error(t, err)
74		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "webhook signature verification is not configured")
75	}
76	
77	func TestVerifyWebhookSignature_TamperedPayload(t *testing.T) {
78		secret := "test-webhook-secret-key"
79		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, secret, "")
80	
81		originalPayload := []byte(`{"event_type":"payment_succeeded","content":{"payment_id":"pay_123"}}`)
82		mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, []byte(secret))
83		mac.Write(originalPayload)
84		validSig := hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil))
85	
86		// Tamper with the payload.
87		tamperedPayload := []byte(`{"event_type":"payment_succeeded","content":{"payment_id":"pay_EVIL"}}`)
88		err := svc.VerifyWebhookSignature(tamperedPayload, validSig)
89		require.Error(t, err)
90		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "invalid webhook signature")
91	}
92	
93	func TestGetPlans_ReturnsThreeTiers(t *testing.T) {
94		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, "", "")
95		plans := svc.GetPlans()
96	
97		assert.Len(t, plans, 3)
98		tiers := make(map[model.PlanTier]bool)
99		for _, p := range plans {
100			tiers[p.Tier] = true
101		}
102		assert.True(t, tiers[model.PlanTierFree])
103		assert.True(t, tiers[model.PlanTierPro])
104		assert.True(t, tiers[model.PlanTierEnterprise])
105	}
106	
107	func TestGetPlanByID_Found(t *testing.T) {
108		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, "", "")
109		plan, err := svc.GetPlanByID("plan_pro")
110		require.NoError(t, err)
111		assert.Equal(t, model.PlanTierPro, plan.Tier)
112		assert.Equal(t, int64(170000), plan.PricePerSeatMonthly)
113		assert.Equal(t, 5, plan.MinSeats)
114	}
115	
116	func TestGetPlanByID_NotFound(t *testing.T) {
117		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, nil, "", "")
118		_, err := svc.GetPlanByID("plan_nonexistent")
119		require.Error(t, err)
120		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "not found")
121	}
122	
123	func TestCreatePaymentIntent_HyperswitchError(t *testing.T) {
124		hsClient := &mockHyperswitchClient{
125			createPaymentFn: func(_ context.Context, _ *hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest) (*hyperswitch.PaymentResponse, error) {
126				return nil, assert.AnError
127			},
128		}
129	
130		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, hsClient, "", "")
131	
132		pi, err := svc.CreatePaymentIntent(context.Background(), "org-123", model.CreatePaymentIntentRequest{
133			Amount:   5000,
134			Currency: "INR",
135		})
136		require.Error(t, err)
137		assert.Nil(t, pi)
138		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "failed to create Hyperswitch payment")
139	}
140	
141	func TestSubscriptionStateMachine_FreePlan(t *testing.T) {
142		// Free plan subscriptions should be created without calling Hyperswitch.
143		hsClient := &mockHyperswitchClient{
144			createPaymentFn: func(_ context.Context, _ *hyperswitch.CreatePaymentRequest) (*hyperswitch.PaymentResponse, error) {
145				t.Fatal("Hyperswitch should not be called for free plan")
146				return nil, nil
147			},
148		}
149	
150		svc := service.NewBillingService(nil, nil, hsClient, "", "")
151		// Note: CreateSubscription requires a DB pool for RLS transactions.
152		// We verify plan lookup and Hyperswitch client selection logic here.
153		plan, err := svc.GetPlanByID("plan_free")
154		require.NoError(t, err)
155		assert.Equal(t, model.PlanTierFree, plan.Tier)
156		assert.Equal(t, int64(0), plan.PricePerSeatMonthly)
157		assert.Equal(t, 0, plan.MinSeats)
158	}
159	
160	func TestDefaultPlans_FeatureLimits(t *testing.T) {
161		plans := model.DefaultPlans()
162	
163		// Free tier limits.
164		free := plans[0]
165		assert.Equal(t, 5, free.MaxUsers)
166		assert.Equal(t, 2, free.MaxWorkspaces)
167		assert.Equal(t, 3, free.MaxKBs)
168		assert.Equal(t, int64(500), free.MaxStorageMB)
169		assert.Equal(t, 1, free.MaxConcurrentVoiceSessions)
170	
171		// Enterprise = unlimited.
172		enterprise := plans[2]
173		assert.Equal(t, -1, enterprise.MaxUsers)
174		assert.Equal(t, -1, enterprise.MaxWorkspaces)
175	}

commit d8a5752a35a46b6e882e7705233896254c833aa5
Author: Jobin Lawrance <jobinlawrance@users.noreply.github.com>
Date:   Sat May 16 20:03:44 2026 +0530

    feat(billing): migrate to per-seat INR pricing with annual billing cycle (#601)
    
    Closes #591, #592.
    
    - Replace flat-rate USD Plan.PriceMonthly with per-seat INR
      Plan.PricePerSeatMonthly (paise) and Plan.MinSeats
    - Pro: ₹1,700/seat/month (min 5 seats)
    - Enterprise: ₹3,500/seat/month (min 20 seats)
    - Add Subscription.SeatCount and Subscription.BillingCycle
    - Annual billing charges 10× monthly per-seat (2 months free ≈ 20% off)
    - Hyperswitch payments now use INR currency
    - Min-seat validation rejects subscriptions below plan threshold
    - Migration 00041 adds seat_count and billing_cycle columns
    
    Signed-off-by: Jobin Lawrance <jobinlawrance@gmail.com>
```
